          .             /**

        .           // Stored as a map of power to coefficient; zero-coefficient terms are erased.
        .           //
        .           // OPTIMISATION: sparse storage matters here. Kauffman bracket polynomials have
        .           // O(n) non-zero terms despite spanning a power range of O(n), so a dense array
        .           // would waste time and space on zero entries.

          .               // Apply one smoothing to the arc connectivity (in-place union-find update).
          .               //
          .               // Each crossing is visited twice around the knot circle; pos = (p, q) are

          .               // position p enters from before_p = p-1, and similarly for q.
